NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
TUESDAY, January 22, 2013 
 
PRESENT: Sylvia Allen, Supervisor; Dawnafe Whitesinger, Supervisor; David Tenney, Supervisor; and Jesse 
Thompson, Vice-Chairman were present in person. Jonathan M. Nez, Chairman was present telephonically.  
9:07 a.m.  Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation was led by Supervisor Tenney. 
CALL TO THE PUBLIC: Karen MacKean addressed the board to invite the Board of Supervisors to an event 
that the White Mountain Conservatives  and the Rural Arizona Conservatives  will be having on February 16th 
from 1:00 to 4:00 pm regarding United Nations Agenda program and Second Amendment Rights.  The meeting 
will be held at the Celebrations Restaurant, upstairs meeting hall in Snowflake at 432 South Main Street. 
CONSENT AGENDA: a) Minutes: January 8, 2013  b) Constable Reports: Holbrook Precinct #1, November; 
Winslow Precinct #2, December; Snowflake Precinct #3, December; Show Low Precinct #5, December; and 
Pinetop-Lakeside Precinct #6, December  2012 c) Justice Court Reports: Holbrook #1, December; Winslow #2, 
December; Snowflake-Taylor #3, December; Kayenta #4, December; Show Low #5, December; and Pinetop -
Lakeside #6, December 2012  d) Juvenile Probation Report December 2012  e) Clerk of Superior Court Report 
December 2012  f) Conduct all mail election for the City  of Winslow on May 12, 2013 to propose a levy 
question on the ballot  g) Approve the re -appointment of Robert Van Wyck and Dan Healy as Superior Court 
Judge Pro Tempores h) Warrant & Voucher Report over $1000 December 2012:  Supervisor Tenney made a 
motion to authorize the Chairman to sign the items in the consent  agenda as presented ; motion seconded by  
Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
UNITED FOOD BANK : Certificate of Appreciation to Navajo County Employees : Steve Arey made a 
presentation from the United Food Bank to Navajo County Employees for fighting hunger by donating to the 
Tate’s Auto Holiday Food Drive.  He indicated that Navajo County Employees contributed  111,987 pounds of 
food, providing 86,144 meals for our community. James Jayne recognized Sheriff Clark, Commander Garcia, 
Lt. Land and the  jail team who collected  19 boxes and $80.00 . Commander Garcia recognized and thanked all 
his team that made this possible . Supervisor Thompson thanked the other teams  of Navajo County  who 
participated for their generosity. 
HUMAN RESOURCES:  Consideration and possible approval of Personnel Actions : Kimberly Eavenson  
reviewed the 17 item personnel action report. Supervisor Tenn ey made a motion to approve the  personnel 
action report as presented; motion seconded by  Supervisor Whitesinger . Vote unanimous approving the 
motion.  
WHITE MOUNTAIN YOUTH CORP:  
a. Presentation by the White Mountain Youth Corp :  Mike Gaffney, Adam Reeck and Steve Campbell  
have been working on launching the White Mou ntain Youth Corp in this area.  WMYC will work to 
empower young people by engaging them in community service, job training and education, 
environmental stewardship and adventure experiences. Mr. Reeck spoke of the needs of the youth in our 
communities and g ave a brief presentat ion. Steve Campbell  spoke about the Whit e Mountain Youth 
Corp and helping  young people in our communities.  Supervisor Allen inquired about the program. Mr. 
Gaffney said he will try and run two crews with 8 to 10 members of the youth; he addressed the funding 
for the contract with work that they  will do. Mr. Reeck spoke about  the recruitment and application

<!-- page 2 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 01-22-2013 
Page 2 of 5 
 
process. Supervisor Whitesinger, Supervisor Allen and Supervisor Thompson thanked Mr. Gaffney, Mr. 
Reeck and Mr. Campbell f or championing our youth and  extended their appreciation  on behalf of our 
community. 
b. Consideration and possible approval of the sale of a vehicle to the White Mountain Youth Corp for the 
sum of $1.00 : Mary Springer discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to approve 
the sale of a vehicle to the White Mountain Youth Corp for the sum of $1.00 ; motion seconded by   
Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Mr. Gaffney thanked the County Manager 
and Supervisor Whitesinger for their support.  
COOPERATIVE EXTENSION:  Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution Number 04-13, 
encouraging Governor Jan Brewer to provide her support to the budgetary request of the University of Arizona 
Cooperative Extension : Michael Larson and Steve Campb ell reviewed the item. Supervisor Allen made a 
motion to adopt Resolution Number 04 -13, encouraging Governor Jan Brewer to provide her support to the 
budgetary request of the University of Arizona Cooperative Extension; motion seconded  by Supervisor 
Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.   
NAVAJO COUNTY PUBLIC HEALTH SERVICES DISTRICT: Board of Directors Session:  Supervisor 
Tenney made a motion  to enter into the Navajo County Public Health Services District Board of Directors  
Session; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
a. CONSENT AGENDA:  
i) Renewal of a Professional Services Agreement with Debra Smalley for Family Planning Services  
ii) Professional Services Agreement with Kristi Wahl for Nursing Services 
iii) Renewal of a Professional Services Agreement with Mauriah Walker for Nursing Services 
iv) Memorandum of Understanding between Navajo County Public health Services District and Kayenta 
Unified School District effective October 1, 2012 to September 31, 2015 
Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve the consent agenda as presented ; motion seconded 
by Supervisor Allen. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible approval of a Subcontract for Nutrition Services with St. Michael’s 
Association for Special Education and the Navajo County Public Health Services District in the amount 
of $24,954.00 for October 1, 2012 through September 30, 201 3 funded through the Arizona Department 
of Health Services, Arizona Nutrition Network Grant : Mary Tyler discussed the item. Supervisor 
Tenney made a motion to approve the  Subcontract for Nutrition Services with St. Michael’s 
Association for Special Education and the Navajo County Public Health Services District in the amount 
of $24,954.00 for October 1, 2012 through September 30, 2013 funded through the Arizona Department 
of Health Services, Arizona Nutrition Network Grant ; motion seconded by  Supervisor Whi tesinger. 
Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
c. Consideration and possible approval of a Subcontract for Nutrition Services with TséHooTsooí Medical 
Center and the Navajo County Public Health Services District in the amount of $14,432.00 for October 
1, 201 2 through September 30, 2013 funded through the Arizona Department of Health Services, 
Arizona Nutrition Network Grant : Mary Tyler discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger  made a 
motion to approve the Subcontract for Nutrition Services with TséHooTsooí M edical Center and the 
Navajo County Public Health Services District in the amount of $14,432.00 for October 1, 2012 through 
September 30, 2013 funded through the Arizona Department of Health Services, Arizona Nutrition 
Network Grant; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
d. Consideration and possible approval of a Memorandum of Understanding between Navajo County 
Public Health Services District and Navajo Nation Child Care Development Fund for provision of 
nutrition and oral health services effective July 1, 2012 to June 30, 2013: Mary Tyler discussed the item.

<!-- page 3 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 01-22-2013 
Page 3 of 5 
 
Supervisor Tenney  made a motion to approve the Memorandum of Understanding between Navajo 
County Public Health Services District and Navajo Nation Child Care Deve lopment Fund for provision 
of nutrition and oral health services effective July 1, 2012 to June 30, 2013 ; motion seconded by  
Supervisor Allen. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
e. Consideration and possible approval of a Memorandum of Agreement between Navajo County Public 
Health Services District and Navajo Nation Head Start effective January 1, 2013 to June 30, 2013: Mary 
Tyler discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger  made a motion to approve  the Memorandum of 
Agreement between Navajo County Public Hea lth Services District and Navajo Nation Head Start 
effective January 1, 2013 to June 30, 2013 ; motion seconded by  Supervisor Allen . Vote unanimous  
approving the motion.  
Supervisor Tenney made a motion to return to regular session ; motion seconded by  Supervisor Nez. Vote 
unanimous approving the motion.  
SHERIFF: Consideration and possible approval of an agreement between the Navajo Nation and the Sheriff’s 
Office for cross certification of Navajo County Sheriff’s Office deputies on the Navajo Reservat ion: Sheriff KC 
Clark di scussed the item.  Chairman Nez thanked the Sheriff and County Attorney and indicated that this 
agreement is a supplement to the Navajo Nati on Officer Model Agreement and benefits all the tribes in Navajo 
County as well. Chairman Nez made a motion to approve the agreement between the Navajo Nation and the 
Sheriff’s Office for cross certification of Navajo County Sheriff’s Office deputies on the Navajo Reservation ; 
motion seconded by  Supervisor Tenney . Vote unanimous approving the moti on.  Supervisor Ten ney 
congratulated Sheriff Clark, Council Delegate Elmer Begay  and Claudia Jackson .  Elmer Begay thanked the 
County Attorney and law enforcement for being there for them.  Supervisor Thompson indicated this is historic 
for Navajo County.  
TREASURER:  
a. Consideration and possible approval of Personal Property Tax abatements per A.R.S. §42 -19118: 
Manny Hernandez discussed the item and property to be abated . Sheriff Clark indicated that some of 
these properties are not livable. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve Personal Property Tax 
abatements per A.R.S. §42 -19118; motion seconded by  Supervisor Allen. Vote unanimous approving 
the motion. 
b. Consideration and possible renewal of bond on the County Treasurer per A.R.S. §42 -18001 B: Manny 
Hernandez discussed the item. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve renewal of bond on the 
County Treasurer per A.R.S. §42 -18001 B ; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger . Vote 
unanimous approving the motion.   
FINANCE:  
a. Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution Number 05-13, approving the form of the 
Equipment Lease Agreement with Zions First National Bank, Salt Lake City, Utah; finding that it is in 
the best interests of Navajo County to enter into said Agreement ; and authorizing the execution and 
delivery thereof: Assessor Cammy Darris and Mary Springer discussed the item. Cammy indicated that 
we will save $59,000 over the next four years. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to adopt Resolution 
Number 05-13, approving the form of the Equipment Lease Agreement with Zions First National Bank, 
Salt Lake City, Utah; finding that it is in the best interests of Navajo County to enter into said 
Agreement; and authorizing the execution and delivery thereof ; motion seconded by Supervisor Allen. 
Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible approval of Amendment #1 to pay Colorado Custom Ware, Inc. $488,368.46 
for the balance owed on the 10 year lease purchase agreement : Mary Springer discussed the item.

<!-- page 4 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 01-22-2013 
Page 4 of 5 
 
Cammy thanked finance and Brad Carlyon for the work they did on this.  James Jayne thanked James 
Menlove and Cammy Darris. Supervisor Tenney made a motion  to approve Amendment #1 to pay 
Colorado Custom Ware, Inc. $488,368.46 for the balance owed on the 10 year l ease purchase 
agreement; motion seconded by Supervisor Allen.  Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
c. County Finance Update : James Menlove introduced Gus Lundberg  who is new  in the Finance 
Department. Gus Lundberg addressed  the Board. Mr. Menlove gave an up date of the  County finances 
and answered questions of the Board . James Jayne , Supervisor Allen and Supervisor Tenney made 
comments.  
 
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:  Discussion and possible approval of 2013 Board Committee Appointments to 
various org anizations and committees: Melissa Buckley  reviewed the Board Committee Appointments. 
Supervisor Tenney  made a motion  to approve the 2013 Board Committee Appointments to various 
organizations and committees ; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the 
motion.  
COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE : Report from Board members, County Attorney and County Manager : 
Chairman Nez reported from Washington DC where  he attended the President’s Inauguration and reported on 
meetings with Legislators regarding Navajo County issues including the Winslow Levee, transportation and 
school bus routes. Supervisor Allen reported on District III indicating that she is getting information regarding 
a possible fire district in Cedar Hills; a possible flooding issue in Joseph City; visited with county fair officials 
Mike Sample, County Fair Board President and Mina Henning, Event Director; visited the Health Department 
in Show Low; and reported on the Town Hall on Feb 16 th. She will work with CSA and Legislators  regarding 
Senator Crandall’s and Senator Jackson’s  intoxication bill to help with treatment programs in Navajo, Apache 
and Coconino County. Supervisor Whitesinger reported on District V thanking James Jayne and Homero Vela 
for getting the street  lighting in Cibeque  complete; she has  worked with Bill Bess  to get information for a 
meeting with the White Mountain Democrats regarding the Pat Mullen cinder pit; she is working with McNary 
community members  to revitalize the community and the McNary Library as a place to meet; she reported on 
the Sunrise Board who is  working with an organization on  a feasibility study to revitalize the economy ; she 
attended the Pinetop City Council Meeting and reported on the First Things First Board meeting. Supervisor 
Tenney reported on the Opening D ay of the Legislature and the Governor’s budget proposal and its impact to 
counties. He will present at  the Senate Natural Resource s Committee hearing to discuss what ECO’s roll has 
been and continue to work for the counties with CSA and the Legislators to help our budget and citizens.  
County Attorney Brad Carlyon discussed that he and Judge Higgins are hoping to get a Veteran’s Court running 
by spring. He reported on the bullying video contest. The videos are outstanding and are on You -Tube and 
indicated the winner this year came from Holbrook. Yesterday James  Jayne, David Tenney  and the Carlyon 
family attended King Volunteerism Day at Show Low which was a good way to set an example to our kids and 
community to help our  neighbors. Supervisor Thompson attended the Chinle Agency and Tuba City elected 
official’s Oath of Office. His granddaughter was born that day. He reported on the Red Dog graveling meetings 
with Kayenta and Pol acca Consolidated villages.  He t hanked the help given to aid the Hopi water issue; h e 
attended the Moenkopi Elderly Facility grand opening; and he reported on the Legislative Opening Session. He 
indicated that he  will attend the  Winslow Chamber meeting to update information concerning the Winslow 
Levee. Supervisor Tenney reported his son Cody who is a senior at Show Low was honored as one of seven  
Arizona football players named to  the United Dairy C ouncil and Arizona Coaches  Association All American 
Team.  Supervisor Thompson indicated that he is very thankful for the young people doing such wonderful 
things and being in the hearts of so many.

<!-- page 5 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 01-22-2013 
Page 5 of 5 
 
ADJOURN: At 10:52 a.m. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to adjourn; motion seconded by  Supervisor 
Allen. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
 
 
 
 
APPROVED: 
 
Jonathan M. Nez, Vice Chairman 
 
 
ATTEST:  
 
 
 
 
DATE:  February 12, 2013 
 
Melissa W. Buckley - Clerk of the Board
